Shin Megami Tensei IV: Apocalypse

Spoiler-free 100% completion for the original Nintendo 3DS release · 44 areas · 1243 tracked items

This page shows everything, including exact locations. It exists so you can search for one specific collectible and find it. If you are playing blind, use the interactive spoiler-free version instead — it hides locations until you ask for them, one item at a time.

Smartphone App record

HintBank App Points until the next dependency appears.
WhereFind Skill Expansion 1 in the smartphone's App purchase list.
Full detailIt appears in the first purchasable App list. Spend 30 App Points when the row becomes available, then verify that Skill Expansion 1 is installed. Report fallen-Hunter markers and use lawful point-card entitlements before assuming the normal level-up supply can cover the entire directory.

Protected Partner Slot 1 (missable)

Partner progression record

HintRotate available support partners instead of relying on a single favorite.
WhereUse the support character occupying partner slot 1 after that slot becomes available.
Full detailBuild the partner gauge through ordinary battles, trigger that slot's Assist Attack at least once, and verify both its short and long battle counters in the DDS ledger. The character name is withheld so the guide never announces a future ally before the game does.
